Michael Kremer Named World Bank Chief Economist 
• Michael Kremer, Nobel laureate and University of Chicago professor, will become the 
World Bank Group’s Chief Economist from October 1, 2026 , succeeding Indermit 
Gill, who retired recently. 
• Kremer, 61, received the 2019 Sveriges Riksbank Prize in Economic Sciences  jointly 
with Abhijit Banerjee and Esther Duflo  for their experimental approach to alleviating 
global poverty. 
• He will lead the World Bank’s research activities, focusing on development challenges 
and evidence-based solutions. 
• His research covers education, healthcare, agriculture, finance, water and innovation 
in developing countries, including programmes on deworming, vaccines and small-farmer 
productivity. 
• Kremer holds a PhD from Harvard University  and is currently a professor at the 
University of Chicago, where he also directs the Development Innovation Lab.

• He replaces Lokhande Prashant Sitaram, who was holding additional charge following the 
transfer of former Chairperson Rahul Singh in June 2026. 
• Before his appointment, Bhandari served as Principal Secretary to the Lieutenant 
Governor of Jammu and Kashmir. 
• He also held additional responsibilities in the Shri Amarnathji Shrine Board, Mata Vaishno 
Devi Shrine Board and the Board of J&K Bank. 
Government Appoints Dinesh Pant and Girija Subramanian as IRDAI Members 
• Dinesh Pant and Girija Subramanian assumed charge as Whole -Time Members of the 
Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority of India (IRDAI) in September 2026. 
• Their appointments were approved by the Appointments Committee of the Cabinet in June 
2026. 
• Dinesh Pant, former Managing Director of LIC, was appointed Whole -Time Member 
(Actuary). 
• Girija Subramanian, former Chairman -cum-Managing Director of The New India 
Assurance Company Limited, was appointed Whole-Time Member (Distribution). 
• Both have been appointed for five years from the date of assuming charge, until attaining 
65 years of age or until further orders, whichever is earlier. 
• IRDAI was established in 1999 and is headquartered in Hyderabad, Telangana.

National Sports Day 2026 – August 29 
 National Sports Day is observed annually in India on 29 August to commemorate the birth 
anniversary of hockey legend Major Dhyan Chand. 
 The Government of India declared 29 August as National Sports Day in 2012, and the first 
observance was held in the same year. 
 The 2026 celebrations were led by Union Minister Mansukh Mandaviya at Major Dhyan 
Chand National Stadium, New Delhi. 
 The theme was “Ek Ghanta Khel Ke Maidan Mein”, encouraging citizens to devote at least 
one hour to sports and physical activity.

International Day of the Victims of Enforced Disappearances 2026 – August 30 
 The United Nations observes the International Day of the Victims of Enforced 
Disappearances every year on 30 August. 
 The day aims to honour forcibly disappeared persons and promote justice, truth and 
accountability. 
 The UN General Assembly adopted Resolution A/RES/65/209 on 21 December 2010, 
declaring 30 August as the observance day. 
 The first observance was held on 30 August 2011. 
National Small Industry Day 2026 – August 30 
 National Small Industry Day is observed annually in India on 30 August to recognise the 
contribution of Small-Scale Industries and MSMEs. 
 The observance commemorates a comprehensive policy package for Small Scale 
Industries announced by Prime Minister Atal Bihari Vajpayee on 30 August 2000. 
 The Government of India designated 30 August as National Small Industry Day. 
 The first National Small Industry Day was observed on 30 August 2001. 
International Day for People of African Descent 2026 – August 31 
 The United Nations observes the International Day for People of African Descent annually 
on 31 August. 
 The day recognises the contributions of people of African descent and promotes efforts to 
combat discrimination. 
 The theme for 2026 was “Expanding the Human Rights of People of African Descent.” 
 The day was established through UNGA Resolution A/RES/75/170, adopted on 16 
December 2020, and was first observed in 2021. 
National Nutrition Week 2026 – September 1–7 
 National Nutrition Week (NNW) is observed annually in India from 1 to 7 September to 
promote awareness about balanced diets and healthy eating habits. 
 The initiative aims to improve nutrition awareness and encourage better health outcomes. 
 National Nutrition Week has been celebrated in India since 1982 by the Food and Nutrition 
Board. 
 The observance is currently spearheaded by the Ministry of Women and Child 
Development. 
World Coconut Day 2026 – September 2 
 World Coconut Day is observed annually on 2 September to highlight the importance of 
coconuts in agriculture, nutrition and livelihoods. 


 
 
 
Monthly Beepedia September 2026 
154 
 
 The 2026 theme is “Coconuts in Times of Uncertainty: Securing Food, Energy, and 
Livelihoods.” 
 The day was first observed in 2009 and was initiated by the Asian and Pacific Coconut 
Community, now known as the International Coconut Community. 
 The date marks the foundation day of the International Coconut Community, established 
in 1969, with India as a founding member. 
National Teachers’ Day 2026 – September 5 
 National Teachers’ Day is observed annually in India on 5 September to honour the 
contribution of teachers. 
 The day marks the birth anniversary of Dr. Sarvepalli Radhakrishnan, India's first Vice -

 Teachers' Day was first celebrated on 5 September 1962. 
 In 2026, President Droupadi Murmu conferred National Teachers Awards on 48 school 
teachers and 21 teachers/faculty members from higher education institutions and 
polytechnics. 
International Day of Charity 2026 – September 5 
 The United Nations observes the International Day of Charity annually on 5 September. 
 The day commemorates the death anniversary of Mother Teresa, who died on 5 
September 1997. 
 The UNGA designated the day through Resolution A/RES/67/105 in December 2012. 
 The first International Day of Charity was observed globally in 2013.

International Day of Democracy 2026 Observed on September 15 
• The International Day of Democracy (IDD)  is observed annually on 15 September to

• The International Day for the Preservation of the Ozone Layer, also known as World

Resolution A/RES/49/114 in 1994.

World Water Monitoring Day 2026 
• World Water Monitoring Day  is observed on 18 September to raise awareness about 
water quality and encourage public participation in monitoring water resources. 
• It was established in 2003 by the American Clean Water Foundation.

Water Association in 2006, while EarthEcho International took over coordination in 2015.

World Alzheimer's Day 2026 – 21 September 
• World Alzheimer's Day is observed every year on 21 September to raise awareness about 
Alzheimer's disease and other forms of dementia. 
• The 2026 theme was “The Earlier You Know, The More You Can Do: A Dementia 
Diagnosis Matters.” 
• Alzheimer's Disease International (ADI) first observed the day in 1994. 
• September is observed as World Alzheimer's Month, established in 2012 to promote 
dementia awareness, education and support.

International Day of Peace 2026 – 21 September 
• The United Nations International Day of Peace was observed on 21 September 2026 
under the theme “Invest in Peace – For Everyone, Everywhere, Every Day.” 
• The day promotes peace among nations and peoples and highlights the importance of 
non-violence. 
• The UN General Assembly first declared the observance in 1981 through Resolution 
A/RES/36/67. 
• In 2001, Resolution A/RES/55/282 fixed 21 September as the annual observance and 
declared it a day of global ceasefire and non-violence. 
• The symbol of the day is a dove carrying an olive branch, representing peace and freedom. 
World Rhino Day 2026 – 22 September 
• World Rhino Day was observed on 22 September 2026 to raise awareness about 
rhinoceros conservation and the threats faced by rhino populations. 
• The observance originated as an initiative of WWF South Africa in 2010. 
• Conservationists Lisa Jane Campbell of Zimbabwe and Rhishja Cota-Larson of the United 
States helped develop it into an international observance. 
• The first international celebration took place on 22 September 2011. 
World Rose Day 2026 – 22 September 
• World Rose Day, also known as the Day for the Welfare of Cancer Patients, was observed 
on 22 September 2026 to honour cancer patients, survivors and caregivers. 
• The day commemorates Melinda Rose, a 12-year-old Canadian girl diagnosed with a rare 
blood cancer who inspired others through her writings and positive outlook. 
• Global estimates cited in the source indicate 20.6 million new cancer cases and 9.8 million 
cancer deaths in 2024. 
• Global annual new cancer cases are projected to reach 34.4 million by 2050.

161 
 
• In India, estimated cancer cases increased from 14.97 lakh in 2023 to a projected 15.70 
lakh in 2025. 
National Ayurveda Day 2026 – 23 September 
• National Ayurveda Day was observed on 23 September 2026 under the theme “Ayurveda 
for a Healthier Tomorrow.” 
• The Ministry of AYUSH celebrated the 11th Ayurveda Day in Nagpur, Maharashtra,

replacing the earlier variable date based on Dhanteras. 
World Car-Free Day 2026 – 22 September 
• World Car-Free Day is observed annually on 22 September to encourage people to reduce 
car usage and promote alternative modes of transport. 
• The idea of reducing car use gained momentum during the 1973 oil crisis and following 
the 1994 Accessible Cities conference in Toledo, Spain. 
• Reykjavik, La Rochelle and Bath were among the first cities to adopt the initiative. 
• The first international Car -Free Day was held in 1999 in Europe as a pilot project under 
the European Union's In Town Without My Car campaign. 
• The initiative later evolved into European Mobility Week, observed from 16 to 22 
September. 
International Day of Sign Languages 2026 – 23 September 
• The United Nations International Day of Sign Languages was observed on 23 September 
2026 under the theme “Declaring Deaf People's Human Rights.” 
• The day promotes the linguistic identity and cultural diversity of deaf people and sign 
language users worldwide. 
• The United Nations General Assembly adopted Resolution A/RES/72/161 on 19 
December 2017, designating 23 September as the observance date. 
• The first International Day of Sign Languages was observed on 23 September 2018. 
• The date commemorates the establishment of the World Federation of the Deaf (WFD) in 
1951. The blue ribbon symbolises the Deaf community's efforts for equality and 
recognition of national sign languages. 
National Service Scheme Day 2026 – 24 September 
• National Service Scheme (NSS) Day is observed annually on 24 September to 
commemorate the establishment of the NSS, a youth development programme under the 
Ministry of Youth Affairs and Sports. 


 
 
 
Monthly Beepedia September 2026 
162 
 
• The NSS was formally launched on 24 September 1969 by Dr Vijayendra Kasturi Ranga 
Varadaraja Rao, then Union Minister of Education. 
• It was initially launched in 37 universities across India. 
• The concept was proposed by Major General Jagannath Rao Bhonsle to encourage 
student participation in community service and nation-building. 
• The year 1969 marked the birth centenary of Mahatma Gandhi, who was born in 1869. 
World Maritime Day 2026 – 24 September 
• World Maritime Day is observed annually on the last Thursday of September to highlight 
the importance of maritime transport and its contribution to global trade and sustainable 
development. 
• In 2026, it was observed on 24 September. 
• The theme for 2026–27 is “From Policy to Practice: Powering Maritime Excellence.” 
• The day was first observed on 17 March 1978, commemorating the entry into force of the 
International Maritime Organization (IMO) Convention in 1958. 
• The IMO, a specialised agency of the United Nations, leads the annual observance. 
World Pharmacists Day 2026 – 25 September

contribution of pharmacists to healthcare and community well-being.

Federation (FIP).

World Tourism Day 2026 – 27 September 
• World Tourism Day is observed annually on 27 September to highlight tourism's social, 
cultural, political and economic significance.

• The United Nations World Tourism Organization established World Tourism Day in 1980,

International Day for the Total Elimination of Nuclear Weapons – 26 September

2013, establishing the observance. 
• The resolution followed the UN's first high-level meeting on nuclear disarmament.

Obituaries 
 
Legendary Gujarati Music Composer Gaurang Vyas Passes Away 
 Legendary Gujarati music composer, arranger and singer Gaurang Vyas passed away at 
the age of 87 in Ahmedabad, Gujarat. 
 He composed music for over 100 films during his career. 
 His notable works include Bhavni Bhavai, Maniyaro, Nasibni Balihari and Liludi Dharati. 
 He received around 11 state-level Best Music Direction awards and a Gujarat government 
Lifetime Achievement Award in 2022. 
Two-Time Grammy Winner Cassandra Wilson Passes Away 
 Renowned jazz singer Cassandra Wilson passed away at the age of 70 in Jackson, 
Mississippi, USA. 
 She was a two-time Grammy Award winner, receiving the awards in 1997 and 2009. 
 Her notable albums include New Moon Daughter, Loverly and Coming Forth by Day. 
 She was also recognised as a Jazz Master by the National Endowment for the Arts in 
2022. 
 
Jharkhand Minister Sudivya Kumar Sonu Passes Away 
• Sudivya Kumar Sonu , Jharkhand's Minister for Urban Development and Higher and 
Technical Education, passed away at the age of 56. 
• He was a Jharkhand Mukti Morcha (JMM) leader and two-term MLA from Giridih. 
• He served as a Cabinet Minister in the Jharkhand government. 
• He handled portfolios including Urban Development, Higher Education and Tourism. 
Hong Kong’s First Post-Colonial Leader Tung Chee-hwa Passes Away 
• Tung Chee-hwa, Hong Kong’s first post-colonial leader, passed away at the age of 89. 
• He served as Hong Kong’s leader from 1997 to 2005. 
• He participated in drafting Hong Kong’s Basic Law in 1985. 
• He later served as Vice -Chairman of the National Committee of the Chinese People’s 
Political Consultative Conference (CPPCC). 
• He was born on 29 May 1937 in Shanghai, China. 
Veteran Tamil Scholar Solomon Pappaiah Passes Away 


 
 
 
Monthly Beepedia September 2026 
165 
 
• Veteran Tamil scholar and orator Solomon Pappaiah passed away at the age of 90 in 
Madurai, Tamil Nadu. 
• He received the Padma Shri in 2021 in the field of Literature and Education. 
• He headed the Department of Tamil at American College, Madurai, from 1961 to 1994. 
• He conducted more than 12,000 Pattimandrams (Tamil debates). 
• He received the Kalaimamani Award in 2000 from the Tamil Nadu Government. 
N. Purushothama Mallya Passes Away 
• Konkani language activist, educationist and writer N. Purushothama Mallya passed away 
on 11 September 2026 at the age of 97. 
• He was awarded the Padma Shri in 2015 in Literature and Education. 
• He contributed to Konkani literature and translated Thirukkural into Konkani. 
• He played a significant role in securing Konkani’s inclusion in the Eighth Schedule of the 
Constitution. 
• He received the Sahitya Akademi Translation Award in 2008. 
 
Pulitzer Prize-Winning Author Annie Dillard Dies at 81 
• Annie Dillard, renowned American author of Pilgrim at Tinker Creek , died at 81 at her 
home in South Wellfleet, Massachusetts. 
• Published in 1974, Pilgrim at Tinker Creek established Dillard as a major voice in American 
literature and won the Pulitzer Prize for General Nonfiction in 1975. 
• Dillard wrote across several genres, including essays, poetry, novels, journalism, 
religious meditations and autobiography. 
• Her notable works include Holy the Firm (1977), Teaching a Stone to Talk (1982) and An 
American Childhood (1987). 
• She was born Meta Ann Doak on April 30, 1945, in Pittsburgh, and later became known 
for her distinctive nature writing and reflections on creation, suffering and spirituality. 
Princess Astrid of Norway Dies at 94 
• Princess Astrid Maud Ingeborg of Norway , elder sister of the late King Harald V, died 
on 11 September 2026 at the age of 94 in Oslo. 
• She served as First Lady of Norway from 1954 to 1968 and performed official duties on 
behalf of the Norwegian Royal Family for several decades. 
• Born on 12 February 1932 in Oslo, she was the second child of King Olav V and Crown 
Princess Märtha. 
• She married businessman Johan Martin Ferner in 1961 and belonged to the House of 
Glücksburg. 
• She was associated with humanitarian and social causes, particularly initiatives 
concerning children, people with disabilities and inclusion. 


 
 
 
Monthly Beepedia September 2026 
166 
 
Former Andhra Pradesh Deputy CM Alla Nani Dies at 56 
• Alla Kali Krishna Srinivas, popularly known as Alla Nani, former Deputy Chief Minister 
of Andhra Pradesh, died at the age of 56 in Visakhapatnam. 
• He was born on 30 December 1969 in Eluru, Andhra Pradesh. 
• He represented the Eluru Assembly constituency, winning elections in 2004 and 2009 
and securing another term in 2019. 
• He later joined the YSR Congress Party (YSRCP)  and became West Godavari district 
president in 2014. 
• He served as Deputy CM and Minister for Health, Family Welfare and Medical 
Education from June 2019 to April 2022. 
Former Union Minister E.M. Sudarsana Natchiappan Passes Away 
• Former Union Minister and senior Congress leader E.M. Sudarsana Natchiappan passed 
away at the age of 78 in Sivaganga, Tamil Nadu. 
• He was born on 29 September 1947 in Eriyur, Sivaganga district. 
• He represented Sivaganga in the 13th Lok Sabha from 1999–2004 and later served two 
terms in the Rajya Sabha from 2004–2016. 
• He served as Minister of State for Commerce and Industry from June 2013 to May 
2014. 
• He was also associated with the Parliamentary Standing Committee on Personnel, Public 
Grievances, Law and Justice. 
 
Padma Shri Awardee Cardiologist G. Vijayaraghavan Passes Away at 84 
• Renowned cardiologist Dr Govindan (G.) Vijayaraghavan passed away on 20 September 
2026 at the age of 84 in Thiruvananthapuram, Kerala. 
• He was awarded the Padma Shri in 2009 in the field of Medicine for his contributions to 
medical science. 
• He established India's first 2D echocardiography laboratory at Government Medical 
College, Thiruvananthapuram, in the late 1970s. 
• He played a key role in establishing KIMS Hospital in 2002, serving as its founding director 
and vice-chairperson and leading its Cardiology Department for over two decades. 
• His research included pioneering work on tropical endomyocardial fibrosis, a heart 
condition prevalent in southern India. 
Vir Chakra Awardee Flight Lieutenant Alfred Tyrone Cooke Passes Away 
• Flight Lieutenant Alfred Tyrone Cooke (Retd.), a decorated Indian Air Force fighter pilot 
and Vir Chakra awardee, passed away in New Delhi in September 2026 at the age of 87. 
• He was commissioned into the Indian Air Force on December 19, 1961, through the 80th 
Pilots Course and served with No. 14 Squadron (The Fighting Bulls). 


 
 
 
Monthly Beepedia September 2026 
167 
 
• During the 1965 India–Pakistan War, he shot down a Pakistani F-86 Sabre while flying a 
Hawker Hunter from Kalaikunda Air Force Station, West Bengal. 
• He received the Vir Chakra, India’s third-highest wartime gallantry award, for his courage 
and leadership during the Battle of Kalaikunda. 
Veteran Malayalam Actress Savithri Sreedharan Passes Away 
• Veteran Malayalam theatre and film actress Savithri Sreedharan passed away in 
September 2026 in Kozhikode, Kerala. 
• She began acting in amateur theatre in 1961 and entered professional theatre in 1984, 
performing with groups such as Kalinga Theatre and Sangamam Theatre. 
• She made her Malayalam cinema debut with Kadavu, directed by M.T. Vasudevan Nair. 
• Her portrayal of Jameela (Umma) earned a Special Jury Mention at the 66th National Film 
Awards and the Kerala State Film Award for Best Character Actress. 
• She received the Kerala State Government Best Theatre Actress Award in 1977 and 1993 
and the Kerala Sangeet Natak Akademi Award for Drama in 2009. 
Italian Football Legend Sandro Mazzola Passes Away 
• Italian football legend Sandro Mazzola, former Inter Milan captain and Italy international, 
passed away in September 2026 at the age of 83. 
• He spent his entire 17-year club career (1960–1977) with Inter Milan, scoring 161 goals in 
565 matches and winning four Serie A titles and two European Cups. 
• He represented Italy in 70 matches, scoring 22 goals, and was part of the team that won 
the 1968 UEFA European Championship. 
• He also played in the 1970 FIFA World Cup final and finished runner-up in the 1971 Ballon 
d'Or. 
• Mazzola was the Serie A top scorer in the 1964–65 season.

SAI, BAI and SAA Sign Pact to Establish Northeast India's First Swimming NCoE 
 The Sports Authority of India (SAI), Badminton Association of India (BAI) and Sports 
Authority of Assam (SAA) signed a tripartite agreement to establish a National Centre of 
Excellence (NCoE) for Swimming in Assam. 
 The Olympic-size swimming pool at Amingaon Sports Complex in Kamrup will be made 
available to SAI for establishing the centre. 
 It will be the first Swimming NCoE in the Northeast region. 
 The centre will identify and nurture swimmers from Assam and other northeastern states 
through specialised coaching and high-performance support.

England Fast Bowler Mark Wood Retires from International Cricket 
• England fast bowler Mark Wood announced his retirement from international cricket in 
September 2026 at the age of 36, ending an 11-year international career. 
• He finished with 119 Test wickets in 38 matches, 80 ODI wickets in 70 matches and 54 
T20I wickets in 38 matches. 
• Wood was part of England’s 2019 ICC Cricket World Cup-winning team, taking 18 wickets, 
and the 2022 ICC Men’s T20 World Cup-winning squad, taking nine wickets. 
• He also featured in England’s 2015 Ashes-winning team and played a key role in the 2023 
Ashes series. 
• In July 2026, he received an Honorary Doctorate of Science from Northumbria University 
for his contribution to British sport.

Bodhana Sivanandan Becomes Youngest Woman Grandmaster at Chess Olympiad 2026 
• Bodhana Sivanandan, an Indian-origin British chess player, became the youngest Woman 
Grandmaster (WGM) in history at the age of 11 in September 2026. 
• She secured her third and final WGM norm while representing England at the 46th Chess 
Olympiad in Samarkand, Uzbekistan. 
• She surpassed Kateryna Lagno's record of 12 years and 4 months, set in 2002. 
• Bodhana achieved her first WGM norm in July 2025, her second in early 2026 and her 
final norm at the Chess Olympiad, scoring 8/11 on Board 1. 
• She won six games at the Olympiad, helping England finish 15th, and also earned her 
second International Master (IM) norm. 
• She previously became the youngest player to represent England internationally at the 
age of nine. 
• She holds the WGM, Woman International Master (WIM) and FIDE Master (FM) titles. 
• V. S. Rathanvel of Coimbatore, Tamil Nadu, became India's 99th Grandmaster after 
crossing the 2,500 Elo rating mark at the 3rd Guwahati Smart City International Open 2026 
in July 2026.
